# Fair Market Rents: Nebraska

- Postal code: NE
- State FIPS: 31
- Counties: 93
- Towns: 0

HUD's Fair Market Rents for Nebraska, fiscal years 1983 to 2027: 93 counties, each with its rents by bedroom count and the FMR areas it has sat in. 6 of them have changed FMR area at least once (not counting 2006, when HUD renumbered every area).
